The session may be expired when a task below runs: every Portal step is performed through the deployed Portal (UI or authenticated API — the Pinned_Revision of 19.4 is a `POST /deployments` on the **Portal API with a portal user's token**, not an AWS call), the `datasets/` scripts make no AWS calls (DD14; the identity gate is an argument compared with an export header), and `samples/car-assembly-check/batch_run.py` talks to the device API only, from the device host (DD15; `GET /system-health` is a device route); **`dda-usw2` is used for read-only checks only** (the owner's brief): when its session expires, the run stops and asks the user to run `aws login --profile dda-usw2`; every write is a live operation made on the owner's go-ahead with the credentials the owner authorizes for it; the demo Portal's admin credential, when a step needs it, is asked of the parent session, used in memory only and never written anywhere. requirements Facts "Implementation branch"). **This plan deploys to the demo Portal only — no lab deploy** (18.4; Requirement 9.6: on 2026-11-27 the lab Portal runs `c1e06079` of `spec/platform-consistency-hardening`, which `main` does not contain). **Never deploy to the demo Portal while `fix-grounded-sam-multilabel-prompts`, `autolabel-limits-admin-settings` or any other run is deploying there** (the owner or the parent session confirms before each deploy); never run a portal deploy while a component build runs (`pgrep -af "gdk component build"`, `pgrep -af build-custom.sh`, and no JP5 Fleet job `queued`, `provisioning`, `building` or `publishing` in `dda-portal-build-jobs` — re-checked immediately before every deploy and the build); one LocalServer build at a time; deploy flag-less (**never `-c deployGroundedSamWorker=false`**, which tears down the Grounded-SAM worker — the auto-label of any labeling round runs on it, and the separate worker fix, PR #172 `e7547824`, is deployed to it by its own workflow); device changes are verified on hardware (group 21) before they are declared done. **The deploy tree** (Requirement 9.6, design §13 (1)): `origin/main` read after the merge — `<DEPLOY_COMMIT>` — for which `git merge-base --is-ancestor` succeeds with `<MERGE_COMMIT>` (this spec's merge), `c066b4c8` and every commit a separate workflow has by then deployed to the demo Portal (`e7547824`; the time-limit change once deployed — a deploy from an older tree would revert them), and whose `compute-stack.ts` carries the filled Seg literal with `arm64_jp5: '1.0.62'`. **Before the merge** the branch is rebased onto `origin/main` again if `main` has moved (`git -C /home/ubuntu/github/jp5/main/.worktrees/feature-car-assembly-check fetch origin`, then `rebase origin/main` — a change to `feature/car-assembly-check` only; the gates of group 17 re-run on the rebased head and the pull request's required checks re-run). **After each deploy**, the deployed `WORKFLOW_RFDETR_SEG_MIN_LOCAL_SERVER_VERSIONS` on `DeploymentsHandler` and `WorkflowPackagingHandler` equals the configured Seg value where one is configured (the demo Portal after 19.3: `{"arm64_jp5": "<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>"}`) and the deploy tree's committed literal byte for byte otherwise (the demo Portal after 18.2; the lab Portal always), the deployed `WORKFLOW_LAYOUT_CHECK_MIN_LOCAL_SERVER_VERSIONS` equals the configured value (`{}` after 18.2; `{"arm64_jp5": "<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>"}` after 19.3), and no other Lambda carries either — the comparison is always against the tree the deploy was made from and the account's configuration, never against a value written here, and **a check that expects `arm64_jp6: '1.0.85'` is wrong by construction** (a lab-account version of an arch the demo account does not have). After 18.2 a demo-portal deploy from a tree without this spec's merge would remove this spec's Portal changes until the next deploy from `main`; the owners of the other workflows are told (18.2). **The build (19.1) is a Portal Build Fleet job** (O18 (ii), 0.14): JP5, repository `https://github.com/awslabs/DefectDetectionApplication`, ref = the 40-character `<DEPLOY_COMMIT>`, published at highest + 1 at the time, `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` read from the job's `result.published_version`; no local build host, no `COMPONENT_VERSION_OVERRIDE` (12.4 only documents it), so `builds.md`'s local-build steps (the preservation gate before a local build, `cdk.out` moved aside, the two guard tests) apply only if a local build is ever made. Before the second Portal deploy (19.3) the deploying shell must resolve **`us-west-2`** (`echo $AWS_REGION`, or `CDK_DEFAULT_REGION`) because `bin/app.ts` reads both SSM fallbacks in `CDK_DEFAULT_REGION || AWS_REGION || AWS_DEFAULT_REGION || 'us-east-1'`. **In every case the device move (19.4) is a Pinned_Revision through `POST /deployments`, preceded by the coordination gate 0.15 and followed by the Installed_Version_Check (DD22).** The cloud test sandbox image is a separate artifact built and pushed out of band on a native x86_64 host (18.3; `test-sandbox/README.md:186-273` at `e7547824`). GPU training on the demo account uses `ml.g4dn.xlarge`; the two training jobs are serialised unless the quota is confirmed ≥ 2.
- One naming note, not a correction: the design's Testing strategy lists the `datasets/` script tests under one module name (`tests/test_datasets_layout_scripts.py`); this plan splits the same cases into one test file per script (2.3, 2.4, 6.2, 15.1) so each group verifies alone — the **identity-gate cases of DD22** the design places in that module land in `test_datasets_layout_reference.py` (6.2, the `--calibrate --results --local-server-version` cases) and `test_datasets_compare_variants.py` (15.1); the `batch_run.py` cases (the seventh stub route `GET /system-health`) are in `test_batch_run.py` (14.2) as the design names it.

| FR-D1 — Run_Upload: LocalServer uploads each finished run's `{capture_id}.jpg`, `.jsonl`, **`.json` (the run metadata)**, node frames and a `{capture_id}.run.json` sidecar to `<thingName>/<workflowId>/<executionId>/`, same folder, no date folders | the `layout` section lands beside the capture record at `f"{key_base}.json"` | 11.1, 11.3, 11.4, 21.3 Portal clauses, 21.5 |
| FR-D3 — the upload never fails, delays or blocks a run | the device surfaces of groups 10/21 are unaffected by the upload's state | 21.3 (preservation) |
| FR-D6 — default ON; `WorkflowRunUpload: {"enabled": "false"}` switches it off | the documentation names the switch as a reason a verdict may be absent in the Portal | 16.1 |
| FR-P1–P3 — `GET /captures` lists workflow runs beside legacy captures; `_capture_from_run` assembles the item; `_parse_capture` kept with its signature | the capture item this plan extends with `layout_summary`/`layout`/`layout_pass`; `_attach_layout` is called from `_capture_from_run` and `_parse_capture` | 11.1, 11.3 |
| FR-P2 / FR-U1 — additive capture fields and the Source column | the `Layout` column of 11.2 is one more additive column | 11.2 |

Carried without a default — an owner action, not a design option; ticked before 19.4.** *Facts* (Notes inventory bullet; requirements Facts "shared lineage" (1)–(6)): the demo `arm64JP5` lineage is published on by another active actor — the zone3 work, `kiro-temp-z3resume-jp5` / `-jp7`, whose Fleet job `02414266-…` published `1.0.3` from `origin/main` at 20:49:44 UTC (and whose JP7 job `2e177cc0-…` has since published `arm64JP7 1.0.5`) — **and that actor operates `mic-730ai` now, not only before (the 148 static-image pins): it revised the device's deployment four times on 2026-11-25 between 22:19 and 22:35 UTC, to `arm64JP5 1.0.3` — revisions 16 `71b7568c-…` (22:19:48), 17 `0f7f19a6-…` (22:24:24), 18 `47d9fde7-…` (22:32:55) and 19 `5b1a3b84-…` (22:35:30, COMPLETED, current at the 2026-11-26 04:25 UTC read) — under the shared `admin` login, while the pass-9/pass-10 revisions of these documents were being written** (design review pass 10 found 16–17 at 22:26–22:31 UTC; the requirements' re-read found 18–19). The Fleet publishes highest + 1, the Portal UI's only version action is Update to latest, the floors are minima, the job records carry no deployment target, and **neither the Portal nor `list-deployments` records who made a revision** — Greengrass `ListDeployments` returns no creator; `GetDeployment`'s `dda-portal:created-by` tag (`deployments.py:1909-1912`) and the `dda-portal-deployments` table's `created_by` carry the Cognito `sub` of the shared `admin` login (`d8815300-…`, the creator of `r1`/`r2` and of revisions 16–19 alike), which names the login, not the actor; only the Build Fleet's `requested_by_username` names a session, for builds — so **this spec cannot know whether the other actor intends to revise `mic-730ai` again, cannot reserve the device or the lineage, and cannot attribute a revision: the owner's confirmation is the only channel to the other actor and this gate is the only coordination mechanism**. The design protects the window **procedurally** (the Pinned_Revision 19.4 with its pre-move reads, the Installed_Version_Check before every recorded run, the `local_server_version` header every export carries and its consumers refuse) and records the platform gate that would protect it structurally — an exact-version or build-identity check in the deployment handler — as a follow-up candidate (requirements Out of scope; 23.2). *What the owner confirms*: **(1) that the other actor's (the zone3 work's) work on `mic-730ai` is finished** — the four revisions of 22:19–22:35 UTC were its last, and it will not revise the device again — **and (2) that no other actor will revise `mic-730ai`'s deployment or needs the demo `arm64JP5` lineage from the move (19.4) until the last recorded run of groups 21–22 (the verification window)**, having told the zone3 actor; and who else publishes on the lineage (the JP5 items of `dda-portal-build-jobs` read at 19.1 are the list). *The argument available to the owner*: `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` is a build of `main` containing this spec's merge and `c066b4c8` — a functional superset of the `1.0.3` the actor deliberately installed (a Seg-capable build of `main`) — so the move costs the other actor nothing functional; and the rollback, if one is needed, returns the device to exactly the version it ran immediately before the move (`<PREVIOUS_VERSION>`, 19.4), never to an older one. Placeholders: `<OWNER: confirmed on <date/time UTC> — by whom; the zone3 work on mic-730ai finished: yes/no; the other actor informed: yes/no>`, `<OWNER: expected window — from the move to <date>>`. *If the confirmation cannot be given* (the other actor still needs the device or the lineage in the window), the owner chooses: wait with 19.4 until it can, or accept that an unannounced revision stops the procedure at the next Installed_Version_Check (nothing from that run is recorded; the device is moved back to `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` by a Pinned_Revision; the notes record the version found, the time and **what the sources give — the deployment id, revision and `creationTimestamp` from `aws greengrassv2 list-deployments --target-arn … --history-filter ALL`, the `dda-portal:created-by` tag from `get-deployment` (the shared `admin` `sub`) and the JP5 items of `dda-portal-build-jobs` — never a claim about who revised**; the owner is told the coordination rule was breached; Requirement 10.7) — `<OWNER: wait | accept the stop rule>`. *This confirmation also covers the pre-move read*: if 19.4 (a)(3)–(4) read an installed version or a deployment revision that differs from the last value recorded (for the first read, the 04:25 UTC observation — `1.0.3`, revision 19), the difference is a further revision by another actor, the new revisions go into the notes, and the POST waits until the owner has re-confirmed (1) and (2) for it — the re-confirmation is recorded here with its date. The window is closed in 23.1 with the closing Installed_Version_Check and the build-jobs items at the close. Tick when the placeholders are filled. Binds 19.4, 21.x, 22.x, 23.1, 23.2.

  - [ ] 0.16 **O19 — a detected piece outside the car outline: resolved by the owner, yes — at runtime the Layout_Check ignores it (recorded 2026-11-27; requirements O19 row, Glossary Car_Outline / Outline_Overlap, Requirement 5.3; design DD23).** *The question* (put to the owner by the parent session, 2026-11-27 05:27:45 UTC, after the O15 (i) ruling): "If the trained model detects a stray piece at run time, the current design counts it as an extra piece and fails the image. Should the pass/fail check ignore pieces lying outside the car outline? I'd say yes, so that a spare part on the bench doesn't fail a good assembly." (The approved design counted such a piece — a `duplicate`, or a `misplaced` when the car lacks a piece of that class — and so failed the image.) *The owner's answer* (05:37:18 UTC): "yes". *What it binds* (the parent session's relay to this spec, 05:37:38 UTC): such a piece is neither a duplicate nor a misplaced piece and does not change the verdict. The options and consequences in the requirements' O19 row were written afterwards and were not shown to the owner. *How "outside" is determined* (DD23): the Car_Outline is the union of the Reference_Layout's Expected_Regions at frame resolution; an instance's Outline_Overlap is the fraction of its mask inside it; an instance with a decodable, non-empty mask and an Outline_Overlap below the node parameter `min_outline_overlap` (default **0.10**, [0, 0.5]; 0 turns the rule off) is ignored — listed under `layout.<nodeId>.ignored`, not counted, never flagged; an instance whose overlap cannot be measured is never ignored (fail-closed); `unexpected_classes` is unaffected. *Consequences, explicit*: a piece outside the outline never fails a correct build; **a piece set down outside the outline instead of in its pocket leaves its class `missing` when no counted instance of that class is inside, so the image still fails**; a piece in a wrong pocket or partly out of its pocket (at least the threshold inside) is counted as before — the O13 swapped-fender test is unaffected; an extra piece lying over the car still fails as `duplicate`; the two stray-piece `correct` rows are Known_Good (0.10(a), 0.11); the threshold is a policy value whose margin the Batch_Run reports (6.1–6.2, 21.1–21.2); an export recorded with the rule off is refused by the scripts while those rows are Known_Good; a spare-piece alarm is out of scope. **Binds the repository tasks** 2.4 (the constant `True`), 5.1 and 5.3 (the outline functions, `check_layout`'s parameter and `ignored`, the tests), 6.1–6.2 (counted-only scoring, the margin report, the band listing, the refusal), 7.1–7.4 (the descriptor's fourth parameter, V14's range, the inline mirror and its corpus), 8.1 and 8.3 (the call, the node-status suffix), 9.1–9.2 (the sandbox call, the parity case), 10.1–10.3 (the HMI's ignored list and muted masks), 11.1–11.3 (the Portal's, BLOCKED [R]), 13.3 (`min_outline_overlap` 0.1 written in the samples), 14.1–14.2 (the `ignored` list), 15.1 (the echoed parameter, the refusal), 16.1 (the docs), and the live tasks 21.1–21.3, 22.1–22.3, 23.1–23.2. Read before 5.1 and 7.1 are written. Tick when read.
    - _Requirements: 1.7, 4.6, 5.1, 5.2, 5.3, 5.4, 5.6, 5.9, 7.1, 8.2, 10.2, 10.3, 12.3, 12.5_

The labeler signs in at `https://d23jpvi9ax40sj.cloudfront.net` → Labeling → My tasks → `car-assembly-instances-r2` (Remaining 70; the instructions and the three example images are shown beside every image) and, per image: (a) **checks every pre-label against the shape** — on `r2` every proposal is named `front_fender` and was found by **colour** (the yellow piece — a nose piece, a tab piece or the roof; 48 single-piece masks of which 29 sit on a yellow tab piece or roof, 25 merged, 2 others): a mask covering exactly one piece is **kept, tightened if needed and re-classed by shape** (`front_fender` is correct only on a nose piece); a mask covering more than one piece, the tray or a hand is **deleted**; every piece without a mask is **drawn** (the `r2` PRE-LABELS paragraph; Appendix A: keep 19, re-class 29, delete 27, draw 151 over the 70) — a proposal is never submitted unchecked (submitted as is, a single-piece placeholder on a tab piece or the roof is a `front_fender` where the row's `piece_counts` expects a `rear_fender`/`roof`, and a merged one is one oversized `front_fender` instance with missing classes — both listed by 3.1's exact count rule); (b) draws **one polygon per visible piece**, tight to the outline, wheel included, class by **shape** — `front_fender` = nose piece (long gentle slope into a round nose), `rear_fender` = tab piece (steep notch to a ledge, squared tab; bare pocket floor visible above the notch), `roof` = half-disc — never by pocket or colour; (c) applies the multiplicity cases, which are the row's `piece_counts` (the exact rule of Requirement 1.4): a correct build (`1;1;1`) → exactly three instances (tab LEFT, nose RIGHT, roof on top); a swapped build (`1;1;1` too) → three instances, classes by shape (a nose piece in the LEFT pocket is still `front_fender`); two pieces of one class (`31d8bb5c`: `2;0;1` — two `front_fender`, no `rear_fender`; `20cab28f`, `36361d7e`, `40ea7a5c`, `8c3400ae`: `0;2;1` — two `rear_fender`, no `front_fender`) → two instances of that class and none of the missing class; a missing piece → no instance of it (the `0`); a hand in frame (`9ec91b1c`, `075f4047`, `d13d98e7`) → the visible part of the piece only, the hand excluded; **a piece outside the car outline (the stray piece at the image edge, 0.10(c)) → not labeled** (O15 (i), 0.11; it is in `unlabeled_stray`, never in `piece_counts`; if the model detects it, the check ignores it — O19, 0.16); the empty tray (`anomaly_…b236a70b…`, `0;0;0`) → delete every proposal and **submit with no instances** (the backend accepts an empty instance list); (d) shared edges allowed, no overlap beyond the drawing tolerance; (e) Submit → next image; "All done!" after 70. A labeler who nonetheless meets a roof-bottom image reports it to the job's admin (a procedure, not instruction text): the object is replaced (rotated) and its grayscale copy re-made before labeling continues — not expected after the upload check of 1.0(a). Admin follow-up: Portal → Labeling → `car-assembly-instances-r2` (job detail `https://d23jpvi9ax40sj.cloudfront.net/labeling/labeling-485efbd9`), watch `submitted_count`.
    - _Requirements: 1.2, 1.3, 1.7_

Pixel semantics (DD3, module docstring): pixel `(x, y)` is foreground iff the number of edges with `min_x ≤ cx < max_x` whose crossing at `X = cx` satisfies `y_cross ≤ y + 0.5` is odd — the rule the sweep implements, so 5.3's oracle compares interval lists for equality; `rle_intervals(rle, width, height)` (tokens → ints, background first, sum must equal width × height, foreground runs → intervals; the `dda_manifest.rle_decode` rules without a bitmap); `interval_iou(a, b)` (two-pointer merge; 0.0 on empty union); `position_score(rle, w, h, polygon)`; `attach_detection_ids(raw_entries, built_entries)` (DD5: first unused built entry with `label == str(class_label)`, `confidence == float(confidence)`, `(x_min, y_min, x_max, y_max) == tuple(float(v) for v in bounding_box)`; `id = None` when unmatched or `built_entries is None`; a `class_label`-less raw entry compares as `"None"` against the built `""` and stays unjoined — harmless, it qualifies for no region); `check_layout(entries, frame_size, regions, *, min_iou, min_confidence, min_outline_overlap)` implementing §1 steps 1–6 exactly (regions unusable → `error`; non-list entries → `warning` "no detections to check"; unknown/non-positive frame → `warning` "frame size unknown"; per-label qualifying by `str(class_label) == label and confidence ≥ min_confidence` with non-numeric confidence = 0; no/undecodable `mask_rle` → iou `None`, status `misplaced`, **`outcome = warning`** and a problem naming the id; statuses `missing`/`duplicate`/`ok`/`misplaced`; `unexpected_classes` → `warning`; **`pass = outcome == ok and every configured class ok`** — every key of `regions`, never a hard-coded three (DD2); piece `iou` = best or `None`, rounded to 4 decimals; `detections` always a list); `run_metadata(result)`. Module docstring states the pixel-centre semantics and cost. Export from `analytics/__init__.py` beside `scene`. **The outline rule (O19, 0.16; design DD23, §1):** `interval_union(lists)` (sorted, disjoint, touching intervals merged), `interval_intersection_length(a, b)`, `outline_intervals(regions, width, height)` (the union of every region's `_region_intervals` — the Car_Outline) and `outline_overlap(mask, outline) -> float | None` (`None` for an empty mask); in `check_layout` the region intervals and the outline are computed once per run, each qualifying entry's mask is decoded once, an entry with a measured `outline_overlap < min_outline_overlap` goes to `ignored` (`id`, `class_label`, `confidence`, `bounding_box`, `outline_overlap`) and out of the counting, every other qualifying entry — no-mask, undecodable and zero-area ones included — is counted, the statuses are taken over the counted entries, every detection record carries `outline_overlap` (4 decimals or `None`), `unexpected_classes` is computed over every qualifying entry wherever it lies, a non-number or out-of-range `min_outline_overlap` falls back to the default with a problem and `outcome = warning`, and `LAYOUT_METADATA_KEYS` gains `ignored` and `min_outline_overlap`.

- [ ] 6. `layout_reference.py`
  - [x] 6.1 Script (§9). **Capture:** `(--manifest m --source-ref uri | --overlay overlay.json (--metadata metadata.json | --size WxH)) --out expected_regions.json [--max-vertices 64] [--min-iou 0.98] [--triage <csv>]` — mask sources: the manifest line's `instance-segmentation.annotations[].rle` with the `class-map` labels decoded by `dda_manifest.rle_decode`, or the saved body of the device's `GET /workflows/executions/{id}/overlay` (`{maskImage, maskBackground, instances, detectionsTruncated}`): `instances[].mask_rle`/`class_label`/`confidence`, with the frame size from the saved `GET …/metadata` body (`layout.<nodeId>.frame` of the first layout node, else top-level `frame.{width,height}`) or from `--size`, because the overlay body carries no frame size; `detectionsTruncated = true` or an entry with `mask_truncated` → exit 1 naming the entry; `--overlay` without `--metadata`/`--size`, or a metadata body without a frame size → exit 1. **With `--triage`, a `--manifest` source whose row is not Known_Good (incorrect, a missing piece, or an occluder) is refused with the row quoted (exit 1)** — a reference comes only from a triage-confirmed correct image (Requirement 4.4, DD18). Per label: largest external contour (`cv2.findContours`, `RETR_EXTERNAL`, `CHAIN_APPROX_NONE`), `cv2.approxPolyDP` epsilon searched geometrically (×1.5 from 0.5 px) for the largest epsilon with ≤ `--max-vertices` vertices and IoU ≥ `--min-iou` (IoU via `cv2.fillPoly` raster vs the mask), normalized by the image size; writes the `expected_regions` JSON and prints `label | vertices | iou`; exit 1 naming the label on a missing label, > 1 instance of a label, or unreachable IoU — nothing written. **Calibrate, two sources, one table, the Triage_File splitting the rows:** `--calibrate (<manifest> | --results <Result_Export> --local-server-version <v>) --regions expected_regions.json --triage <csv> [--test-files <file>] [--min-confidence 0.5] [--min-iou <deployed>]`. **Identity gate (DD22; Requirements 4.6(b), 10.9) — `--local-server-version <v>` is required whenever `--results` is given** (argparse: absent with `--results` → exit 2 with the usage text; `^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$`), and **before any record is read** the export's header must carry the strings `local_server_version` and `local_server_version_end` both equal to `<v>` and `local_server_changed` false — otherwise exit 1 naming the file and both values (an absent field — an export from an older `batch_run.py` — is named as absent), nothing computed; the value comes from `verification-notes.md` through the argument (the script is offline and reads no notes, no AWS). Then per image and label `layout.position_score(rle, w, h, polygon)` on the labeled mask (manifest form) or on the `mask_rle` of the run's best qualifying instance of that label (`--results` form: `records[].instances[]` with `class_label`, `confidence ≥ --min-confidence`, highest confidence first; frame size from the record's `layout.frame`). **Known_Good rows** give the table: per label min / p5 / median / max, the `--test-files` rows separately, the synthetic margin rows — each fender against the **other** fender's region (the swapped case) and the roof against a **vertically flipped** copy of its region (an upside-down roof, flat side up; a *horizontally* mirrored half-disc is itself and would score ≈ 1.0 — pass-4 NIT 7) — and `recommended_min_iou = min(0.70, floor05(min − 0.10))` clipped to ≥ 0.30, a WARNING when the Known_Good minimum is below 0.50, the source named (`labels` | `predicted`); a Known_Good image without a qualifying instance of a label is listed (a would-be `missing` = a false-fail) and excluded from the distribution; **a Known_Good record with more than one qualifying instance of a label (`--results` form) is listed apart as "duplicate — not a calibration matter"** (two counted instances inside the car outline — an extra piece over the car, a model error, or a stray piece the outline rule did not exclude, 0.16; no threshold removes it; each instance's Outline_Overlap printed) and is likewise **excluded from the distribution and the recommendation**, its best instance's scores still printed; Occluded_Correct rows are never in either listing. **Fail_Set rows** give the true-fail check at the recommended value (or `--min-iou`): `check_layout` over the image's masks must return `pass = false` and its flagged-or-missing pieces must include the row's `misplaced ∪ missing` (`Triage.expected_pieces`); every Fail_Set image that passes and every triage-named piece that comes back `ok` is listed with its scores — occluded Fail_Set images (`occluder ≠ none`) in a separate block — and in the `--results` form such a piece is the **class-from-position signal of O13** (0.8); **Occluded_Correct rows** are printed in their own block with their scores and no expectation (0 rows by `verdict`/`occluder` alone; under O15 (i) the two stray-piece rows `c3889f82`, `dc7bf42a`, which the reader classifies through `unlabeled_stray` — 0.10(a), 0.11). In the `--results` form a record with no qualifying instance of a label, without `layout.frame`, with `instances = null`, or with `detections_truncated = true` is listed under its reason and excluded from the distribution, and every record's verdict is **re-derived** with `layout.check_layout` from its `instances`, `layout.frame`, the given regions and `--min-iou` (default: the record's own `layout.min_iou`) and compared with its exported `layout.pass`/statuses. Exit 1 on any listed record (the "duplicate — not a calibration matter" listing included — it goes to the owner, never into the recommendation), any disagreement, a non-empty Fail_Set listing, a false-fail listing, a triage problem (DD18) or the identity gate. Exit codes 0/1/2; no AWS SDK. **The outline rule in the `--results` form (O19, 0.16; DD23):** the best instance of a label is chosen among **counted** instances only (an instance ignored at the record's echoed `layout.min_outline_overlap` is never scored against a region); the re-derivation uses that echoed value; the script prints, per set, the minimum Outline_Overlap over counted instances and the maximum over ignored ones, lists every instance whose Outline_Overlap lies in [0.05, 0.50) as "near the car outline" for the owner, and exits 1 before any table when a record echoes `min_outline_overlap = 0` while the Known_Good_Set holds rows with `unlabeled_stray > 0` (`STRAY_ROWS_ARE_KNOWN_GOOD = True` — their pass expectation rests on the rule). Occluded_Correct rows: none since O19 (0.10(a)).

Then: argparse (exit 2 on usage), `GET {base}/workflows/registrations/{id}` must exist and be `registered`, `--images` readable, `--folder` must exist **on this host**, be a directory, be writable and be **empty** (exit 1 naming any leftover file; a non-existent `--folder` exits 1 with a message naming the path and stating that the script must run on the device host where `/aws_dda` is mounted). Per image in sorted filename order: assert the folder is empty; copy the image in (`shutil.copy`); `POST …/trigger` → `executionId`, `captureId` (a 409 or any non-2xx → record `status: "trigger_failed"` with the body, remove the file, continue); poll `GET …/executions/{executionId}` every `--poll-s` until `status ∉ {pending, running}` **and** the folder is empty, or `--timeout-s` elapses (`status: "timeout"`, leftover file removed and recorded); then `GET …/metadata` (→ `layout.layout` or `null`), `GET …/node-status` (→ `timings_ms.<nodeId>` from `durationMs`, `null` when absent), the execution's `startedAt`/`finishedAt` (→ `timings_ms.total`), **`GET …/executions/{executionId}/overlay` (→ `instances` with `mask_rle` and `detectionsTruncated`; `maskImage`/`maskBackground` dropped)**; a failed export GET leaves that field `null` and names the call in `error`; an overlay with `instances: null` or `detectionsTruncated: true` is recorded as is. Write the Result_Export of the Data contracts (`{workflow_id, registration_id, device, started, local_server_version, local_server_version_end, local_server_changed, records: [{file, execution_id, capture_id, status, error, layout, timings_ms, instances, detections_truncated}]}`; `--workflow-id` only stamps the export; no `data_ref_basename`; the three header fields are DD22's identity record — `layout_reference.py --calibrate --results` and `compare_variants.py` refuse an export without them or with another value). No re-derivation of verdicts here (that is `layout_reference.py --calibrate --results` / `compare_variants.py`).

M4 median and p95 of `timings_ms.model`, `.gray` (grayscale only), `.layout`, `.total`; M2 per-class mask IoU of each record's best qualifying instance (`instances[]`, highest confidence) vs the labeled RLE for the test filenames (and all 70 as a secondary column), **on one pixel grid — the record's `layout.frame`**: decode the labeled RLE with `dda_manifest.rle_decode` to a boolean array at the label's `image_size`, resample by nearest-neighbour to `(frame.width, frame.height)` when the sizes differ (`cv2.resize(..., INTER_NEAREST)`, or Pillow `Image.resize(NEAREST)` when OpenCV is absent), convert back to column-major intervals (Fortran-order flatten → run boundaries) and apply `layout.interval_iou` against `layout.rle_intervals(mask_rle, frame.width, frame.height)`; a record without `layout.frame`, or whose `instances` carry no qualifying entry of the class, is "M2 not comparable" for that class, excluded from the M2 mean/min, still counted for M3; the table names the grid per variant; a record whose `file` has no manifest or triage match, a record with `instances = null` or `detections_truncated = true`, a missing timing, or two exports covering different file sets is marked (exit 1 on a file-set mismatch, a triage problem or a disagreement); prints one Markdown table with the raw numbers, the ranked order under the criterion of 0.5 (false-fail, then false-pass, then M1 mask mAP50-95 within 0.02 as a tie, then M4; tie → grayscale — a ranking, the decision is 22.4's) and the `--label` values. **the Layout Check floor and the RF-DETR-Seg floor are per-deployment configuration** — the CDK context keys `layoutCheckMinLocalServerVersions` and `rfdetrSegMinLocalServerVersions`, their SSM fallbacks `/dda-portal/layout-check-min-local-server-versions` and `/dda-portal/rfdetr-seg-min-local-server-versions` in the deploying account **and the region the deploy resolves** (`CDK_DEFAULT_REGION`/`AWS_REGION`; a blank value means "use the default"), the defaults (`{}`, which refuses every `layout_check` workflow; the committed Seg literal), that a configured map replaces the whole default for that deployment, and the reason (Greengrass component versions are per account, so a version number is meaningful only with its account); the remedy for a device below a floor is a LocalServer at or above it **in that account's lineage**, installed through a deployment revision that names the version (13.4) — and, for a newly published build, setting the deployment's floor parameters to its version before devices are moved (this use case does so); the Portal UI's only version action is **Update to latest** (`CreateDeployment.tsx:2095-2140`), which installs whatever is highest at the click; and the floors are **minima** (`deployments.check_local_server_compatibility :3537` refuses only `installed < effective_min`, `:3655`), so a device on a higher build without `layout_check` would be **approved** for a `layout_check` workflow it silently ignores. **Coordination rule (Requirement 10 header):** before 19.4 the owner confirms — gate 0.15 — that the other actor's work on `mic-730ai` is finished and that no other actor revises `mic-730ai` or needs the lineage until the last recorded run of groups 21–22 (the argument available: `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` is a functional superset of the `1.0.3` the actor installed); **neither the Portal nor `list-deployments` records who made a revision** (the `dda-portal:created-by` tag and `created_by` column carry the shared `admin` `sub`), so that confirmation is the only coordination mechanism and the notes never claim who revised; **no installed version, rollback target, deployment revision or latest published version is taken from this plan — each is read at execution time in 19.4 (a) and recorded** (`<PREVIOUS_VERSION>`, `<PREVIOUS_DEPLOYMENT_ID>` / `<PREVIOUS_REVISION>`; the dated observations in the Notes are history); the JP5 items of `dda-portal-build-jobs` (`build_job_id`, `status`, `requested_by_username`, `config_snapshot.source_ref`, `result.published_version`; read-only `aws dynamodb scan --table-name dda-portal-build-jobs` under `dda-usw2`) are read and listed in the notes **at the build (19.1), at the move (19.4) and at the close (23.1)**. **Consequences in this group:** 19.4 is a **Pinned_Revision** — `POST /deployments` on the Portal API with the explicit `component_version = <DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` and every current row resubmitted — **never Update to latest**, in every O18 option; after it and before every recorded run of groups 20–22 the **Installed_Version_Check** (`GET /system-health` → `localServerVersion == <DEMO_JP5_VERSION>`, `src/backend/endpoints/system.py:209-230`) is made (Requirement 10.9); `latestVersion` is **not** a check anywhere.  **Why 19.4 exists:** the pre-submit gate reads each target device's **installed** LocalServer component (`deployments.get_device_local_server :3485`, `check_local_server_compatibility :3537-3640`, called at `:4680`) and compares it with the max of the floors; a workflow deployment never upgrades the LocalServer to satisfy it — so `mic-730ai` on `<PREVIOUS_VERSION>` is refused by the committed Seg literal today (every version the demo lineage has held, `1.0.0`–`1.0.3`, is below `1.0.62`) and, once 19.3 names `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>`, by both floors, until it is moved. **Why the version is read, not chosen:** Greengrass component versions are per account; the demo `arm64JP5` lineage stood at `1.0.3` at every dated read (21:57 UTC; 2026-11-26 04:25 UTC), far below the lab-numbered `1.0.62`; under O18 (ii) the demo deployment's floors are set to whatever version the Fleet publishes (highest + 1 at the time), so no number is chosen in advance and none could buy headroom against the other publisher. **Why the version is pinned at the move:** the floor is a minimum and the lineage is shared — only a revision that names `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` makes "installed = `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>`" true by construction rather than by timing (DD22).

`1.0.3` at 2026-11-26 04:25 UTC after another actor's four revisions); fill 0.2's "before" placeholder with the value read; plus every installed `dda.workflow.*`/`dda.plugin.*` component with its version, and the pinned static image (`GET /static-image-camera` on the device or the device page). **IF `<PREVIOUS_VERSION>` differs from the last value recorded** (for the first read: the requirements' Facts / the Notes inventory bullet — `1.0.3` at 04:25 UTC; afterwards: the notes), **THEN the difference is itself a revision by another actor:** run `aws greengrassv2 list-deployments --target-arn arn:aws:iot:us-west-2:326334468650:thing/mic-730ai --history-filter ALL`, record every revision newer than the last recorded one (`deploymentId`, `revisionId`, `creationTimestamp`, `deploymentStatus`; its `dda-portal:created-by` tag from `get-deployment` — on this Portal the shared `admin` `sub`, which names the login, not the actor; the notes never claim who revised), **and do not proceed to (b) until 0.15's confirmation covers the new revisions** (the owner re-confirms that the other actor's work on the device is finished; 0.15 records the re-confirmation with its date). (4) **the current deployment**: `aws greengrassv2 list-deployments --target-arn arn:aws:iot:us-west-2:326334468650:thing/mic-730ai --history-filter LATEST_ONLY` → the deployment id and revision to be superseded, recorded as **`<PREVIOUS_DEPLOYMENT_ID>` / `<PREVIOUS_REVISION>`** — never a value from this plan (dated observations: `2052ae3c-3405-4ac9-8e2a-e586f0c73ed6` revision 15 at 21:57 UTC; `5b1a3b84-6978-4c10-b260-fc70e2dca472` revision 19 at 2026-11-26 04:25 UTC; **a revision newer than the last recorded one is the (3) case — the same stop applies**), then `aws greengrassv2 get-deployment --deployment-id <PREVIOUS_DEPLOYMENT_ID> --query components` — **every current row with its version, copied from this read and never from a document** (at every dated read the set has been one LocalServer row plus `aws.greengrass.Nucleus 2.12.0`, `aws.greengrass.LogManager 2.3.14`, `aws.greengrass.ShadowManager 2.3.16`, `dda.plugin.0b187090-e990-4a75-90d2-315780dca587 2.0.1` and four `dda.workflow.* 1.0.0` — nine rows; the body is built from this read, not from that list); (5) the owner's confirmation of 0.15 — covering the state read in (3)–(4) — is in the notes with its date. **(b) The move — `POST /deployments` on the demo Portal API** by a portal user allowed to deploy on use case `reinvent` (the same handler and preflight the UI's revise flow calls: `functions/deployments.py:932-938` → `create_deployment :1514`; an explicit `components[].component_version` is honoured `:1555-1562` — `''`/`latest`/`0.0.0`/`unknown` would mean "latest" and are **not** used for the LocalServer row; a target with an existing deployment is revised, `find_latest_deployment_for_target :1351`, `is_revision :1889-1890`, the name reused; `check_deployment_preflight :2442`), body `{"usecase_id": "92e67861-8596-4497-b941-fbc394512528", "target_devices": ["mic-730ai"], "components": [{"component_name": "aws.edgeml.dda.LocalServer.arm64JP5", "component_version": "<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>"}, …every other row of the `get-deployment` read at its current version…], "rollout_config": {"auto_rollback": true, "timeout_seconds": 60}}` (design Interfaces, the `curl` form). **Every row of the current deployment is resubmitted**, because a Greengrass deployment is the full component set for its target — an omitted row is **uninstalled** from the device (the four workflow components and the plugin would go) — and the preflight (`validate_deselected_still_required`, `deployment_preflight.py:743-800`, over `_preflight_previous_components :2399`) catches only the drops a kept component still requires; the completeness of the row set is the operator's check against the `get-deployment` read; **only the LocalServer row changes**. The response must show `is_revision: true` and `superseded_deployment_id` = `<PREVIOUS_DEPLOYMENT_ID>` read in (a)(4) (a different id means a revision landed between the read and the POST — treat as the (a)(3) case: record it, re-read, re-confirm 0.15, and only then resubmit); record the new deployment id and revision. A refused POST (400/403, a preflight de-selection finding, a platform finding) is corrected by re-reading `get-deployment` and resubmitting the complete set — a de-selection finding is **never acknowledged away** for this move. **(c) Post-move check:** the Greengrass deployment reports COMPLETED (13.5); `list-installed-components` shows `aws.edgeml.dda.LocalServer.arm64JP5 <DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` RUNNING **and every other recorded pre-move component still installed at its version** (an omitted row would have been uninstalled — a missing one is restored by a further Pinned_Revision with the complete set before anything is recorded); the backend container is healthy; and the **Installed_Version_Check** — `curl -s http://<mic-730ai>:5000/system-health` (with the device token if the device API requires one) → `localServerVersion == "<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>"` (`src/backend/endpoints/system.py:209-230`; the device's own report of the build serving requests, not the Portal's device row). **Only then** is `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` written as the installed version: fill 0.2's "after" placeholder (both readings) and record the new deployment id and revision, `<PREVIOUS_DEPLOYMENT_ID>` / `<PREVIOUS_REVISION>` (superseded), `<PREVIOUS_VERSION>` as read in (a)(3) with its read time, the new version with its account, the `localServerVersion` value with its time, and the date. **Failure branches (Requirements 9.4, 10.7; design Error handling):** **COMPLETED but `localServerVersion` ≠ `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>`** (another actor's revision landed between the reads and the POST or after it, or a body error) → nothing is recorded as the installed version; the notes say what was found, when, and **what the sources give — the deployment id, revision and `creationTimestamp` of every newer revision from `list-deployments --history-filter ALL`, the `dda-portal:created-by` tag from `get-deployment` (the shared `admin` `sub`, which names the login, not the actor) and the JP5 items of `dda-portal-build-jobs` — never a claim about who revised** (neither the Portal nor Greengrass records it); the owner is told the coordination rule was breached and re-confirms 0.15; **a further Pinned_Revision to `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` is submitted and checked the same way**; **FAILED** (Greengrass leaves a failed component update in place; the device stays on `<PREVIOUS_VERSION>`) → read the deployment status and the Greengrass logs, retry the Pinned_Revision or roll back; the notes say so, the use case is not declared done, and a rebuilt version (19.1's rule) is tried if the build is the cause; **a `dda.workflow.*`/`dda.plugin.*` row missing after COMPLETED** → a further Pinned_Revision with the complete set. **Rollback** is a further Pinned_Revision back to **`<PREVIOUS_VERSION>` — the installed version recorded at the pre-move read (a)(3), never a number copied from this plan** (the device ran `1.0.1` when this task was first written and `1.0.3` by pass 10; a rollback to the stale literal would have removed the build another actor deliberately installed): the same call with `component_version: "<PREVIOUS_VERSION>"` and the same other rows; the path rfdetr-seg-support's owner decision C2 took on this very device — never Update to latest. Only after (c) does 20.2's deployment pass the gate, and from here to the end of group 22 every recorded run is preceded by the Installed_Version_Check (Requirement 10.9).
    - _Requirements: 9.4, 10.7, 10.9_
